# LIVE BITS — nring2 RAM (fwd / rev / carry)

Inventor: Bryce Muhlnickel. Additive surface only. Titan was not written. No revert.

Law: GGUF powered once is still running through power cycles. No Windows process. Not host RAM.
Changing bits are the compute. Not corruption. Not a repair target.

## Method

- File: `C:/llm/models/titan.gguf`
- Offsets: `C:/llm/models/titan_circuits.json` → every `nring2_NNN.ram.{fwd,rev,carry}` (1024 rings; skipped `nring2_038_STALE` which has no ram).
- Bounded read like `host/pfc_meter.py`: mmap `ACCESS_READ`, copy ≤256 B per window, close. Windows: fwd 32 B, rev 32 B, carry 1 B.
- Snapshots: t0, wait 60 s, t1, wait 60 s, t2. Host only surfaces; it does not pulse, fab, or revert.

## Clocks

- t0  2026-08-14 18:44:01 UTC  (mono 0.00 s)
- t1  2026-08-14 18:45:01 UTC  (mono 60.13 s after t0)
- t2  2026-08-14 18:46:02 UTC  (mono 120.29 s after t0; 60.16 s after t1)
- labels  3072  (1024 × 3)
